}.

At the function level, the denotation ignores statement-level details (captured by the sets
brk
,
ctn
,
rtn
, and
gto
of
Clit.Denote
), and instead focuses on the four resulting behaviors from a given incoming query
(i.e., termination, abortion, silent divergence, and reacting divergence), each capturing the original observable behaviors in CompCert (by the
trace
or
itrace
field of each behavior set).

6.2.
Denotational Semantics of Clight Statements

Overview

We compositionally build the semantics of Clight statements from broken semantics, labeled broken semantics, and finally to the intact semantics, as shown in Table
2
.
Broken semantics
captures the behavior of
a statement
s
under the convention that execution halts immediately upon
encountering a
goto
statement, thereby disregarding the semantics of any
subsequent instructions that the
goto
might target. Building upon this,

labeled broken semantics
interprets
s
from a specified label, but still
within the broken-semantics discipline. Finally, the
intact semantics
of Clight statements is obtained by
systematically composing the (labeled) broken semantics, thus recovering the full
control-flow behavior of
goto
statements.

These semantics are parameterized by an environment
\mathbb{E}
and a semantic oracle
\chi
(
\subseteq\text{\footnotesize cquery}\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times\text{\footnotesize creply}
), where
\mathbb{E}
is a tuple of global environment
\mathbb{g}
and local environment
\mathbb{e}
, and the oracle
\chi
is used for specifying the terminating behavior of function calls from the Clight statement
s
.

Table 2
.
Three related semantic notions of Clight statements.

Semantics

Symbol

Denotation

Brief Description

Broken semantics

\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

Clit.Denote

Halt at
goto
statements.

Labeled broken semantics

\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

\text{\footnotesize label}\to\text{\footnotesize Clit.Denote}

Start from a given label.

Intact semantics

{\{\!\!\{s\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

Clit.Denote

Full semantics via composition.

6.2.1.
Broken Semantics of Clight Statements

Atomic statements

The denotation of atomic statements satisfy the following equations, where
\text{\small expr\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)
means trying to derive the value of
e
under environment
\mathbb{E}
and state
\sigma
.

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{skip}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\mathbb{1}\qquad\llbracket\textbf{skip}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

(11)

\displaystyle\llbracket x=e\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\Set{\big((te,m),\text{\footnotesize nil},(te^{\prime},m)\big)}{\begin{aligned} &\exists v,\text{\small expr\_eval}(\mathbb{E},(te,m),e)=\textbf{Succeed}(v)\\
&\quad\land te^{\prime}=te[x\mapsto v]\end{aligned}}

(12)

\displaystyle\llbracket x=e\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\Set{\big(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil}\big)\mid\text{\small expr\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)=\textbf{Fail}}

(13)

\displaystyle\llbracket x=e\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

(14)

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{return}\ ?e\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize rtn})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil},\sigma,v)}{\text{\small expr\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,?e)=\textbf{Succeed}(v)}

(15)

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{return}\ ?e\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil})}{\text{\small expr\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,?e)=\textbf{Fail}}

(16)

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{return}\ ?e\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

Clight function calls

The denotation of Clight function calls satisfies the following equations, where
\text{\small query\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e_{1},\overrightarrow{e_{2}})
means trying to derive the query of a call from
e_{1}
and
\overrightarrow{e_{2}}
under environment
\mathbb{E}
and state
\sigma
.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\Set{((te,m),\tau,(te^{\prime},m^{\prime}))}{\begin{aligned} &\exists q,\,\text{\small query\_eval}(\mathbb{E},(te,m),e_{1},\overrightarrow{e_{2}})=\textbf{Succeed}(q)\\
&\;\;\land\exists v^{\prime},\,(q,\tau,(m^{\prime},v^{\prime}))\in\chi\land te^{\prime}=te[?x\mapsto v^{\prime}]\end{aligned}}\end{aligned}

(17)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil},q)\mid\text{\small query\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e_{1},\overrightarrow{e_{2}})=\textbf{Succeed}(q)}\end{aligned}

(18)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil})\mid\text{\small query\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e_{1},\overrightarrow{e_{2}})=\textbf{Fail}}\end{aligned}

(19)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}\end{aligned}

Operators for composing denotations

Before showing the semantics of composite statements, we define the following operators for semantic composition. Specifically, for given denotations
D_{1}
and
D_{2}
of
Clit.Denote
, we use
D_{1}\Cup D_{2}
to denote the union of them, and use
D_{1}\fatsemi D_{2}
to denote the sequential composition of them, in which the composition operator
\circ
is overloaded as follows.

\displaystyle(D_{1}\Cup D_{2}).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq D_{1}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup D_{2}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx}),\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

\displaystyle(D_{1}\fatsemi D_{2}).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq D_{1}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\circ D_{2}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (D_{1}\fatsemi D_{2}).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq D_{1}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup(D_{1}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\circ D_{2}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})),\;\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\{\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}\}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\qquad\begin{aligned} \text{where}\quad\!&R_{1}\circ Y&&\!\!\!\!\!\triangleq\{(\sigma_{1},\tau)\mid\exists\sigma_{2}\ \tau_{1}\ \tau_{2},(\sigma_{1},\tau_{1},\sigma_{2})\in R_{1}\wedge(\sigma_{2},\tau_{2})\in Y\wedge\tau=\tau_{1}\cdot\tau_{2}\}\\
&R_{1}\circ R_{2}&&\!\!\!\!\!\triangleq\{\left(\sigma_{1},\tau,\sigma_{3}\right)\mid\exists\sigma_{2}\ \tau_{1}\ \tau_{2},\ (\sigma_{1},\tau_{1},\sigma_{2})\in R_{1}\wedge(\sigma_{2},\tau_{2},\sigma_{3})\in R_{2}\wedge\tau=\tau_{1}\cdot\tau_{2}\}\\
&R_{1}\circ V&&\!\!\!\!\!\triangleq\{\left(\sigma_{1},\tau,\sigma_{3},v\right)\mid\exists\sigma_{2}\ \tau_{1}\ \tau_{2},\ (\sigma_{1},\tau_{1},\sigma_{2})\in R_{1}\wedge(\sigma_{2},\tau_{2},\sigma_{3},v)\in V\wedge\tau=\tau_{1}\cdot\tau_{2}\}\end{aligned}

Clight control flow

The denotations of Clight control flow statements (i.e., conditional branching, sequential statements,
break
,
continue
,
return
, and Clight loops) satisfy the following equations, where
\text{\small bool\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)
meaning trying to derive the Boolean value of the Clight expression
e
under environment
\mathbb{E}
and state
\sigma
. We write
\Uparrow X
to lift a set of states
X
into a binary relation with empty trace,
and
\nabla R
to project a ternary relation
R
onto a binary one by canceling its target state.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize tss})}\;(\subseteq\text{\footnotesize state})\triangleq\Set{\sigma}{\text{\small bool\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)=\textbf{Succeed}(\textbf{T})}\\
&\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize fss})}\;(\subseteq\text{\footnotesize state})\triangleq\Set{\sigma}{\text{\small bool\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)=\textbf{Succeed}(\textbf{F})}\\
&\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize err})}\;(\subseteq\text{\footnotesize state})\triangleq\Set{\sigma}{\text{\small bool\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)=\textbf{Fail}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\Uparrow X\;\triangleq\;\{\,(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil})\mid\sigma\in X\,\},\qquad\quad\,\text{ for }X\subseteq\text{\footnotesize state},

\displaystyle\nabla R\;\triangleq\;\{\,(\sigma,\tau)\mid\exists\sigma^{\prime},\,(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime})\in R\},\text{ for }R\subseteq\text{\footnotesize state}\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times\text{\footnotesize state}.

\displaystyle\llbracket s_{1};s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\triangleq\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\fatsemi\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\llbracket\textbf{ if}(e)\ s_{1}\textbf{ else }s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\textbf{test}(\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize tss})})\circ\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup\textbf{test}(\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize fss})})\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx}),\\
&\qquad\qquad\;\;\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\llbracket\textbf{ if}(e)\ s_{1}\textbf{ else }s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\;\\
&\qquad\qquad\;\;(\Uparrow\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize err})})\cup\textbf{test}(\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize tss})})\circ\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\cup\textbf{test}(\llbracket e\rrbracket_{\mathbb{E}}.\text{({\footnotesize fss})})\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\llbracket\textbf{break}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk})\triangleq\mathbb{1},\text{ and other fields are assigned the empty set}.\\
&\llbracket\textbf{continue}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize ctn})\triangleq\mathbb{1},\text{ and other fields are assigned the empty set}.\end{aligned}

\displaystyle N_{1}=\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize ctn})\quad\quad N_{12}=N_{1}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq&\ N_{12}^{*}\circ(\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk})\cup N_{1}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk}))\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk})=\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize ctn})\triangleq\varnothing

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq&\ N_{12}^{*}\circ(\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup N_{1}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})),\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq&\ N_{12}^{*}\circ\big(\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\cup N_{1}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\cup N_{1}\circ(\nabla\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize ctn}))\big)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg})\triangleq N_{12}^{*}\circ\big(\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket.\text{({\footnotesize fin\_dvg})}\cup N_{1}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket.\text{({\footnotesize fin\_dvg})}\cup(\triangle N_{12})^{\infty}\big)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\triangleq\;&\text{ let }\mathbb{D}=\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\cup\big(N_{1}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\big)\text{ in}\\
&\left((\triangle N_{12})^{*}\circ\blacktriangle N_{12}\right)^{*}\circ(\triangle N_{12})^{*}\circ\mathbb{D}\cup\left((\triangle N_{12})^{*}\circ\blacktriangle N_{12}\right)^{\infty}\end{aligned}

Switch statement

In Clight, a
switch
statement evaluates a case expression
e
and then selects
the execution branch from a list of case statements
\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}
. Each case statement
consists of an optional case label
?n_{k}
and its associated statement
s_{k}
.
If
e
matches a case label, execution begins at that case; if no match is found,
execution starts from the first default case (denoted
(?,s)
); and if neither a
matching case nor a default exists, the switch terminates immediately, behaving as

skip
.
Formally, we use
\llbracket\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
to denote the semantics of sequentially executing
the list of case statements
\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}
one by one, as defined by
Equations (
20
) and (
21
).
We further define
\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n)
to denote execution beginning from the
case labeled
n
, if it exists, or from the default case otherwise.

(20)

\displaystyle\llbracket\text{\footnotesize LSnil}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\triangleq\llbracket\textbf{skip}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

(21)

\displaystyle\llbracket(?n_{1},s_{1});\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\triangleq\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\fatsemi\llbracket\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

(22)

\displaystyle\{\!\!\!\;|{\text{\footnotesize LSnil}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n)\triangleq\llbracket\textbf{skip}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}

(23)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\{\!\!\!\;|{(n_{1},s_{1});\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n)\triangleq\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n),&&\text{ if }n\neq n_{1}\\
&\{\!\!\!\;|{(n_{1},s_{1});\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n)\triangleq\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\fatsemi\llbracket\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}},&&\text{ if }n=n_{1}\end{aligned}

(24)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\{\!\!\!\;|{(?,s_{1});\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n)\triangleq\llbracket s_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\fatsemi\llbracket\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}},&&\ \ \text{ if case }n\text{ not found in }\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\\
&\{\!\!\!\;|{(?,s_{1});\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n)\triangleq\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n),&&\ \ \text{ if case }n\text{ found in }\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\|e\|_{n}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil},\sigma)}{\text{\small switch\_case\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)=\textbf{Succeed}(n)}

\displaystyle\|e\|_{n}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil})}{\text{\small switch\_case\_eval}(\mathbb{E},\sigma,e)=\textbf{Fail}}

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq{\textstyle\bigcup_{n}\Bigl(\|e\|_{n}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\circ\bigl(\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n).(\text{\footnotesize brk})\bigr)\Bigr)}

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq{\textstyle\bigcup_{n}\Bigl(\|e\|_{n}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm}))\circ\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n).(\text{\footnotesize err})\Bigr)}\cup\|e\|_{n}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk})\triangleq\varnothing

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\;&{\textstyle\bigcup_{n}\Bigl(\|e\|_{n}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm}))\circ\{\!\!\!\;|{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}|\!\!\!\;\}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(n).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\Bigr)},\\
&\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}\end{aligned}

Unstructured branching

The denotation of a
goto
statement and a labeled statement satisfy the following equations, where Equation (
25
) and (
26
) reflect the essence of the name
broken
for
\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
.

(25)

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{goto}\ j\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\triangleq\Set{(\sigma,\text{\footnotesize nil},(j,\sigma))}{\sigma\in\text{\footnotesize state}}

(26)

\displaystyle\llbracket\textbf{goto}\ j\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

(27)

\displaystyle\llbracket j:\ s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx}),\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

6.2.2.
Labeled Broken Semantics of Clight Statements

We use
\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)
to denote the semantics of executing the statement
s
from the given label
j
. If there is no such a labeled statement inside
s
, then
\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)
is assigned the empty denotation (i.e., each field of which is the empty set).

\displaystyle\llfloor{s_{1};s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\triangleq\big(\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\fatsemi\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\big)\Cup\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)

\displaystyle\llfloor{\textbf{ if}(e)\ s_{1}\textbf{ else }s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\triangleq\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\Cup\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)

\displaystyle N_{1}^{j}=(\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize ctn}))\qquad N_{12}^{j}=N_{1}^{j}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llfloor{\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\;&\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize brk})\cup N_{1}^{j}\circ\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk})\cup N_{12}^{j}\circ\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\ \cup\\
&\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize brk})\cup\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\circ\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llfloor{\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\;&\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize err})\cup N_{1}^{j}\circ\bigl(\nabla\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize ctn})\cup(\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\fatsemi\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}).(\text{\footnotesize err})\bigr)\ \cup\\
&(\nabla\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize ctn}))\cup\bigl(\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\fatsemi\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\bigr).(\text{\footnotesize err})\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llfloor{\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize brk})=\llfloor{\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize ctn})\triangleq\varnothing\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llfloor{\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\;&\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup N_{1}^{j}\circ\bigl((\llbracket s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\fatsemi\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\bigr)\ \cup\\
&\bigl(\llfloor{s_{2}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\fatsemi\llbracket\textbf{loop }s_{1}\ s_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\bigr).(\text{\footnotesize xxx}),\;\!\!\,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\llfloor{\text{\footnotesize LSnil}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}\\
&\llfloor{(n_{1},s_{1});\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\triangleq\bigl(\llfloor{s_{1}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\fatsemi\llbracket\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\bigr)\Cup\llfloor{\overrightarrow{(?n_{2},s_{2})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\llfloor{\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\llfloor{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\llfloor{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize brk})

\displaystyle\llfloor{\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize brk})\triangleq\varnothing

\displaystyle\llfloor{\textbf{switch}\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\llfloor{\overrightarrow{(?n_{k},s_{k})}}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize xxx}),\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\{\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}\}

\displaystyle\llfloor{j:\ s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j^{\prime})\triangleq\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}\qquad\,\,\text{ if }j^{\prime}=j

\displaystyle\llfloor{j:\ s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j^{\prime})\triangleq\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j^{\prime})\ \ \text{ if }j^{\prime}\neq j

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \textbf{Otherwise }&(\text{i.e., }s=\textbf{skip}\mid\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}\mid x=e\mid\textbf{break}\mid\textbf{continue}\mid\textbf{return}\ ?e\mid\textbf{goto}\ j)\\
&\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})=\varnothing,\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize gto},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}\end{aligned}

6.2.3.
Intact semantics of Clight statements

We define the intact semantics of Clight statement
{\{\!\!\{s\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
by composing the broken semantics
\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
and labeled broken semantics
\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
systematically. To achieve this, we use
\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}
to denote the uncurried form of
\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j)
, such that, for instance,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\subseteq(\text{\footnotesize label}\times\text{\footnotesize state})\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times(\text{\footnotesize label}\times\text{\footnotesize state})

\displaystyle\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize gto})\subseteq\text{\footnotesize state}\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times(\text{\footnotesize label}\times\text{\footnotesize state})

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\triangleq\Set{\bigl((j,\sigma),\tau,(j^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\bigr)\mid\bigl(\sigma,\tau,(j^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})\bigr)\in\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}(j).(\text{\footnotesize gto}))}

The intact semantics of Clight statements are then defined by the following equations, where
\text{\small labels\_of}(s)
denotes the set of statement labels that have appeared in
s
in the form of “
j:...
”.

\displaystyle{\{\!\!\{s\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup\big(\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\circ\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize gto})}^{*}\circ\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize xxx})}\big),\;\text{\footnotesize xxx}\!\in\!\{\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize brk},\text{\footnotesize ctn},\text{\footnotesize rtn},\text{\footnotesize cll}\}

\displaystyle{\{\!\!\{s\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\cup\Big(\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\circ\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize gto})}^{*}\circ\bigl(\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize err})}\cup\text{\small external\_label}(s)\bigr)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\;\;\text{ where }\text{\small external\_label}(s)=\Set{((j,\sigma),\text{\footnotesize nil})}{j\notin\text{\small labels\_of}(s)\land\sigma\in\text{\footnotesize state}}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &{\{\!\!\{s\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg})\triangleq\\
&\quad\ \ \llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}{.(\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg})}\cup\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\circ\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize gto})}^{*}\circ\big(\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.(\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg})\cup(\triangle\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.(\text{\footnotesize gto}))^{\infty}\big)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &{\{\!\!\{s\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\triangleq\textbf{let }G=\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize gto})}\textbf{ in}\\
&\quad\ \ \llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\cup\llbracket s\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize gto})\circ\Big(\bigl((\triangle G)^{*}\circ\blacktriangle G\bigr)^{*}\circ(\triangle G)^{*}\circ\boldsymbol{\langle}{\llfloor{s}\rrfloor_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}}\boldsymbol{\rangle}.{(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})}\cup\bigl((\triangle G)^{*}\circ\blacktriangle G\bigr)^{\infty}\Big)\end{aligned}

6.3.
Denotational Semantics of Clight Functions

A Clight function
F
is defined by Equation (
10
).
We use
\llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}
(of
FDenote
) to denote the semantics of the Clight function
F
with the identifier
id_{F}
. This function-level semantics is parameterized by a global environment
\mathbb{g}
and a semantic oracle
\chi
(
\subseteq\text{\footnotesize cquery}\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times\text{\footnotesize creply}
) for specifying the terminating behavior of the functions invoked by
F
, and satisfies the following equations, where
function_entry
intuitively represents the setup of a function’s internal initial state and local environment at entry,
while
free_local_vars
represents the release of its local resources at exit.

\displaystyle\llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})\triangleq\Set{id_{F}},\text{ i.e., the domain of }F\text{ is a singleton set with the unique element }id_{F}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\Set{\!((id_{F},\vec{v},m),\tau,(m^{\prime},v^{\prime}))\!}{\!\begin{aligned} &\exists\mathbb{e}\;\sigma,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Succeed}(\mathbb{e},\sigma)\land\\
&\quad\;\;\,\exists\sigma^{\prime},\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})\land\boldsymbol{\big(}(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime},v^{\prime})\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize rtn})\;\!\vee\\
&\qquad\qquad\;(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime})\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\land v^{\prime}=\text{\footnotesize Vundef}\boldsymbol{\big)}\;\!\land\\
&\quad\;\,\,\qquad\text{\small free\_local\_vars}(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e},\sigma^{\prime})=\textbf{Succeed}(m^{\prime})\end{aligned}\!}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\Set{((id_{F},\vec{v},m),\tau)}{\begin{aligned} &(1)\,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Fail}\land\tau=\text{\footnotesize nil};\textbf{ or}\\
&(2)\,\exists\mathbb{e}\,\sigma,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Succeed}(\mathbb{e},\sigma)\;\,\land\\
&\qquad\quad\;\;\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})\land(\sigma,\tau)\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize err});\textbf{ or}\\
&(3)\,\exists\mathbb{e}\,\sigma,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Succeed}(\mathbb{e},\sigma)\;\,\land\\
&\qquad\quad\;\;\exists\sigma^{\prime},\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})\land\boldsymbol{\big(}(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime})\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize brk})\ \vee\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\,(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime})\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize ctn})\boldsymbol{\big)};\textbf{ or}\\
&(4)\,\exists\mathbb{e}\,\sigma,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Succeed}(\mathbb{e},\sigma)\;\,\land\\
&\qquad\quad\;\;\exists\sigma^{\prime}\,v^{\prime},\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})\land\boldsymbol{\big(}(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime},v^{\prime})\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize rtn})\ \vee\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\,(\sigma,\tau,\sigma^{\prime})\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\land v^{\prime}=\text{\footnotesize Vundef}\boldsymbol{\big)}\ \land\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\;\text{\small free\_local\_vars}(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e},\sigma^{\prime})=\textbf{Fail}\end{aligned}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})\triangleq\Set{\big((id_{F},\vec{v},m),\tau,q\big)}{\begin{aligned} &\exists\mathbb{e}\,\sigma,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Succeed}(\mathbb{e},\sigma)\ \land\\
&\quad\quad\;\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})\land(\sigma,\tau,q)\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})\end{aligned}}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize$\text{\footnotesize xxx}$ })\triangleq\Set{\>\,\big((id_{F},\vec{v},m),\tau\big)\;\,}{\begin{aligned} &\exists\mathbb{e}\,\sigma,\text{\small function\_entry}(\mathbb{g},m,F,\vec{v})=\textbf{Succeed}(\mathbb{e},\sigma)\ \land\\
&\qquad\;\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})\land(\sigma,\tau)\in{\{\!\!\{s_{F}\}\!\!\}}_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}.(\text{\footnotesize$\text{\footnotesize xxx}$ })\end{aligned}}\end{aligned}

where
\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}
. It is worth noting that the aborting behavior
\llbracket(id_{F},F)\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})
accounts for four possible cases:
(1) abnormal initialization at function entry;
(2) abnormal execution within the function body;
(3) premature exit of the function body via
break
or
continue
;
(4) abnormal deallocation of local variables at function exit.
Besides, aborting and diverging behaviors of a function do not include those of its callees. Instead, we record the function’s call behavior explicitly, so that such cases can be handled later when defining the semantics of open modules.

6.4.
Semantics of Open Modules

Definition of open module

An open module is modeled as a collection of Clight functions, i.e.,
M\triangleq(id_{1},F_{1});...;(id_{n},F_{n})
. Each
F_{i}
may call functions internal or external to module
M
(i.e.,
open
).

Semantic notations

We use
\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}
(of
FDenote
) to denote the semantics of open Clight modules, where the parameter
\mathbb{g}
denotes a global environment, and
\chi

(\subseteq\text{\footnotesize cquery}\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times\text{\footnotesize creply})
is a semantic oracle for specifying the terminating behavior of functions external to module
M
.

Before showing the semantic equations, we overload the symbol “
\Cup
” to denote the union of function-level denotations, and define
\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}
to represent the combined denotation of all functions defined in
M
. Formally, given function-level denotations
D_{1}
and
D_{2}
of
FDenote
, we have:

(28)

\displaystyle(D_{1}\Cup D_{2}).(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\triangleq D_{1}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx})\cup D_{2}.(\text{\footnotesize xxx}),\text{ for }\text{\footnotesize xxx}\in\Set{\text{\footnotesize dom},\text{\footnotesize nrm},\text{\footnotesize err},\text{\footnotesize cll},\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg},\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg}}

(29)

\displaystyle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}\triangleq\llbracket(id_{1},F_{1})\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}\Cup\cdots\Cup\llbracket(id_{n},F_{n})\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}\text{, where }M=(id_{1},F_{1});...;(id_{n},F_{n})

Semantic equations

The semantics of open Clight modules then satisfies the following equations:

\displaystyle\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})\triangleq\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})

\displaystyle\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\triangleq\mu\chi_{0}.\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\chi_{0}\cup\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})

\displaystyle\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})\triangleq\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})^{*}\circ\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize err})

\displaystyle\text{\small ExtnlQuery}(X)\triangleq\{(id,\vec{v},m)\mid id\not\in{X}\}\text{ for }X\subseteq\text{\footnotesize ident}

\displaystyle\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})\triangleq\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})^{+}\circ\textbf{test}\bigl(\text{\small ExtnlQuery}(\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom}))\bigr)

\displaystyle\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg})\triangleq\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})^{*}\circ\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize fin\_dvg})\cup(\triangle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll}))^{\infty}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\triangleq\ &\bigl((\triangle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll}))^{*}\circ\blacktriangle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})\bigr)^{*}\circ(\triangle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll}))^{*}\circ\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize inf\_dvg})\ \cup\\
&\bigl((\triangle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll}))^{*}\circ\blacktriangle\llparenthesis M\rrparenthesis_{\hat{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize cll})\bigr)^{\infty},\text{ where }\hat{\chi}=\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\chi\end{aligned}

Here, the set
\text{\small ExtnlQuery}(X)
denote the set of external queries w.r.t. the given identifier set
X
, so as to capture calls to functions outside the module. Besides, once the normal semantics
\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})
is defined, we extend the oracle
\chi
(specifying the terminating behavior of external functions) to
\hat{\chi}=\llbracket M\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\chi
so as to cover the terminating behavior of both internal and external functions.

6.5.
Semantic Linking

We use
(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}
to denote the semantic linking of two open Clight modules, where
\mathbb{g}
is the same global environment, and the semantic oracle
\chi

(\subseteq\text{\footnotesize cquery}\times\text{\footnotesize trace}\times\text{\footnotesize creply})
specifies the terminating behavior of function calls external to modules
M_{1}
and
M_{2}
. Intuitively, semantic linking merges the domains and behaviors of the two modules while continuing to rely on the oracle
\chi
for any calls external to both. The denotation of semantic linking satisfies the following equations:

\displaystyle(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize dom})}\triangleq\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})\cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize nrm})}\triangleq\mu\chi_{0}.\bigl(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi_{0}\cup\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi_{0}\cup\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize nrm})\bigr)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize err})}\triangleq(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize cll})}^{*}\circ(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize err})}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize cll})}\triangleq(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize cll})}^{+}\circ\textbf{test}\bigl(\text{\small ExtnlQuery}\bigl(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})\cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.(\text{\footnotesize dom})\bigr)\bigr)\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize fin\_dvg})}\triangleq(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize cll})}^{*}\circ(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize fin\_dvg})}\cup\bigl(\triangle C\bigr)^{\infty}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize inf\_dvg})}\triangleq\;&\big((\triangle C)^{*}\circ\blacktriangle C\big)^{*}\circ(\triangle C)^{*}\circ(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize inf\_dvg})}\cup\big((\triangle C)^{*}\circ\blacktriangle C\big)^{\infty}\\
&\text{where }C=(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\Cup\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\tilde{\chi}}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize cll})}\text{, and }\tilde{\chi}=(\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket)_{\chi}^{\mathbb{g}}.\text{({\footnotesize nrm})}\cup\chi\end{aligned}

Theorem 6.1 (Semantic adequacy).

For any Clight modules
M_{1}
and
M_{2}
,

\llbracket M_{1}\rrbracket\oplus\llbracket M_{2}\rrbracket=\llbracket M_{1}+M_{2}\rrbracket
.

6.6.
Csharpminor and Cminor

Syntax of Csharpminor

Csharpminor is an
untyped
low-level imperative language featured with infinite loops, control flow blocks, and premature exits from blocks, which are defined as follows.

(Statements)

\displaystyle u\triangleq\ \begin{aligned} &\textbf{skip}\mid u_{1};u_{2}\mid\textbf{ if}(e)\ {u}_{1}\textbf{ else }{u}_{2}\mid\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}:sig\mid\textbf{goto}\ j\mid j:\ {u}\mid x=e\mid\\
&\textbf{loop }{u}\mid\textbf{block}\{u\}\mid\textbf{exit}(n)\mid\textbf{return}\ ?e\mid\textbf{switch}\ b\ e\ \overrightarrow{(?n_{k},u_{k})}\mid...\end{aligned}

(Functions)

\displaystyle F\triangleq\begin{aligned} (\overrightarrow{x_{k}},\overrightarrow{y_{k}},\overrightarrow{z_{k}},u_{F},sig)\end{aligned}

A Csharpminor function
F
is composed of a list of parameter names
\overrightarrow{x_{k}}
, a list of temporary variables
\overrightarrow{x_{k}}
, a list of local variables
\overrightarrow{z_{k}}
(whose addresses are taken in
F
), a statement
u_{F}
representing the function body, and a signature
sig
specifying the types of the arguments and return value of
F
.

Syntax of Cminor

Cminor functions is defined by the parameter list
\overrightarrow{x_{k}}
, the temporary variables
\overrightarrow{x_{k}}
, the size of memory block for stack space
stacksize
, the function body
t_{F}
, and the signature of
F
.
In Cminor, Csharpminor local variables become sub-blocks of the Cminor stack memory block.

(Statements)

\displaystyle t\triangleq\ \begin{aligned} &\textbf{skip}\mid t_{1};t_{2}\mid\textbf{ if}(e)\ {t}_{1}\textbf{ else }{t}_{2}\mid\textbf{call }?x\ e_{1}\ \overrightarrow{e_{2}}:sig\mid\textbf{goto}\ j\mid j:\ {t}\mid x=e\mid\\
&\textbf{loop }t\mid\textbf{block}\{t\}\mid\textbf{exit}(n)\mid\textbf{return}\ ?e\mid\textbf{switch}\ b\ e\ table\ de\!\!\>f\!ault\mid...\end{aligned}

(Functions)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} F\triangleq(\overrightarrow{x_{k}},\overrightarrow{y_{k}},stacksize,t_{F},sig)\end{aligned}

Semantic domain

Csharpminor and Cminor enjoy the same form of signatures for statement denotations, since they adopt the same block-exit mechanism to model structured control flow.

⬇

}.

The main difference between
Cshm
/
Cmin.Denote
and
Clit.Denote
lies in how they handle structured control flow. While
Clit.Denote
uses
brk
and
ctn
,
Cshm
/
Cmin.Denote

instead employs
blk
; the meanings of the other sets remain the same as in Clight.
The semantic infrastructure (i.e., states, queries, and replies) of Csharpminor and Cminor builds the same with that of Clight, as summarized in Table
1
.

The Cminorgen phase translates Csharpminor to Cminor, where CompCert coalesces the separate memory blocks allocated to local variables into a single block that represents the activation record as a whole.
We write
\llbracket u\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
for the semantics of Csharpminor statements, where

\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},\mathbb{e})
; and write
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\chi}^{\mathbb{E}}
for that of Cminor statements, where
\mathbb{E}=(\mathbb{g},sp)
with
sp
denoting the stack pointer, as shown in Table

. Other notations (i.e.,
\chi
,
\mathbb{g}
, and
\mathbb{e}
) carry the same meaning as in Clight.

The function-level denotational semantics of Csharpminor and Cminor, including those for functions, open modules, and semantic linking, are defined in the same way as in Clight.
